## Execution Mode

- **direct**: Execute units sequentially in the coordinating task. Fresh sub-agents perform the mandatory hostile reviews. Before protected handoff, this task may create and protect only `spoonjoy-delivery` plus dedicated delivery-control-plane GitHub App identities and exact root-reviewed disabled `BrokerFoundationV1` Cloudflare resources; namespace existence alone grants no import or operation authority. It cannot target or consume source-owned web/native, production Cloudflare, ASC, TestFlight, notification, build, secret, or worktree state. Source-repository work begins only after the retained TestFlight task's protected handoff validates.
- **public evidence**: Every repository artifact/output uses repository, random 128-bit stable worktree ID, role, branch, and base SHA only. No local path or unkeyed path-derived digest is public. ID-to-path mappings and optional keyed HMAC integrity commitments exist solely in private Desk cleanup state and are never committed, uploaded, logged, summarized, or embedded through another object. Until Unit 6c is complete, every command transcript and hostile fixture result remains outside the repository in `0600` private ephemeral storage. A generated `PreGateTranscriptManifestV1` keys every code/test unit preceding Unit 6c in this document's exact ordered unit index to its private Desk locator and SHA-256 digest; those units commit code/tests only. Unit 6c scans every exact manifest entry and may then commit only allowlisted summaries/digests; raw bytes are purged after the backfill proof.

## Objective

Build and pilot a production-grade delivery system that carries one Spoonjoy product contract through web/backend, native Apple, and MCP/agent implementations to independently verified exact releases and terminal cleanup. Only a protected `ReleaseSetPublished` append with its mandatory exact post-result `PublicationObserved` proof may declare the Product Change shipped.

- Codex task `019f2e25-2fc3-75b2-8ba3-335f3777115a`: its protected source-owner handoff and receiver acknowledgment are prerequisites for Unit 13b; it retains the active TestFlight/source lane until validation succeeds.

## Completion Criteria

- [ ] `spoonjoy-delivery` has protected `main` and `release-ledger`: main requires zero human approvals, zero bypass, and exact-provenance Delivery Guardian CI/review checks; ledger permits only Ledger Writer bypass. Both enforce no force-push/deletion; merged branches delete automatically; repository defaults are read-only and cannot approve PRs; root-bound `WorkflowTokenPolicyV1` proves every protected-main workflow and repository-owned reusable-workflow edge has a closed effective permission set with no repository-mutating scope or `pull_request_target`; Actions are SHA-pinned; installs deterministic; TypeScript strict; gates zero-warning at 100% statements/branches/functions/lines.
- [ ] Structural schemas and semantic validators reject unknown fields, malformed or duplicate identities, missing edges, cycles, illegal transitions, stale evidence, invalid dispositions, unauthorized actors, non-monotonic generations, competing claims, and ambiguous ownership.
- [ ] Product Contracts capture invariants, state transitions, authorization, ownership, errors, idempotency, ordering, offline behavior, compatibility, degradation, migration, rollback, and scenario expectations.
- [ ] RFC 8785 `semanticContractDigest`, `packDigest`, and `provenanceDigest` boundaries pass duplicate-key, ordering, number, Unicode, changed-projection, unrelated-source, and Swift/TypeScript golden-vector tests.
- [ ] Web emits the exact Photo Studio Contract Pack and exposes its runtime digest; native locks it, validates codecs/scenarios, embeds code-signed provenance, and externally binds app/archive/IPA/ASC identities.
- [ ] A one-time audited `ledger-root-bootstrap-v1` ceremony installs distinct least-privilege Ledger Writer and Delivery Guardian Apps, creates each exact reviewed disabled `BrokerFoundationV1` before App-key import, upgrades those same foundations to operational policy only after authoritative key/policy equality, anchors distinct workflow-inaccessible reviewer-attestation and governance-observer keys, installs `ProtectedReleaseTagV1`, and permanently disables setup after Ledger Writer appends `RootAuthorityInstalled`. Foundation plans bind runtime/storage/routes/bootstrap identity/code/policy/config, exact prestate, response-loss reconciliation, rollback, and teardown; an empty namespace or partial runtime grants no import authority. Ledger Writer is the sole `release-ledger` and canonical release-tag-namespace bypass actor with delivery `contents:write` plus read-only Actions/Checks provenance permissions; its tag broker exposes exact create-if-absent tag and body-only empty-assets Release creation only, with no update/delete/upload route or implicit-tag branch. A root-authorized local three-GET-only observer signs actor/admin/full-bypass/ETag one-use ruleset snapshots before/after tag creation without granting Administration to Ledger Writer. Guardian is zero-bypass and sole exact-provenance required-check actor. The root authorizes one separate journaled `source-guardian-cutover-v1`; handoff requires its exact `SourceGuardianCutoverCompleted` terminal. App keys/tokens remain outside Actions in purpose-specific OIDC brokers; same-Actions-App check spoofing, forged/replayed reviewer envelopes, identity substitution, ordinary `GITHUB_TOKEN`, other Apps, direct, and self-declared writes/checks fail.
- [ ] Static typed operation DAGs authorize all real web/native/governance/provider alternatives; runtime receipts bind authoritative pre-state, hash-linked resolved inputs, selected branch, sanitized result, retries, and containment.
- [ ] Environment-gated jobs use exact documented `environment: {name, deployment: false}` syntax: required reviewers and wait timers still gate the job, custom deployment protection rules are absent as incompatible, and authenticated post-queries prove no GitHub Deployment/status object was created.
- [ ] Production deploy, migrations/capabilities/rollback/canary/reporting, TestFlight upload/metadata/group/notification/expiry, cleanup, artifact/attestation, finalization, projection, and tag paths reject missing, stale, revoked, wrong-SHA/generation/graph/claim authorization.
- [ ] Source-owned attestors use exact reviewed delivery SHA and purpose-specific brokers: ASC reads are GET-only with a dedicated read credential; mutations use a distinct preferred app-limited or explicitly scoped-fallback credential, a per-run claim-bound X25519 recipient, and an exact-operation local broker. Provider keys/JWTs/generic leases never enter Actions or untrusted children. The Transporter TCB is the isolated broker plus the runtime-observed complete code-signed launcher/app/JVM/helper/JAR/library/descendant executable closure with pinned signatures/tree digests, sterile broker-owned `HOME`/`CWD`/`TMPDIR`, one exact private key path, and all alternate searched key locations proven absent or sandbox-inaccessible. Raw output stays private; sanitized attestations are dispatched/verified through authenticated `gh`.
- [ ] Every evidence class passes public/private/forbidden classification and adversarial leak scanning before logs, summaries, annotations, caches, artifacts, commits, or releases.
- [ ] Rebaseline validates exact web/native main SHAs, release-task commit, protected handoff/acknowledgment, zero in-flight source mutations, and cleanup ownership before source edits.
- [ ] Current and previous client identities freeze; previous-source debug/simulator and cleanup plus independent attestation and previous-installed queue seed are direct same-generation predecessors of production promotion. Unit 34 creates generation-bound `InstalledQueueRecoveryAuthorityV1` before seed, leaves it executable through green promotion, and Unit 39 or a causal failure alone terminally reconciles and retires it; no failure/checkpoint or fresh seed may bypass a nonterminal queue. Exact source config pins `preview_urls:false` and `workers_dev:false`; a closed authoritative `WorkerInvocationSurfaceV1` covers deployment/traffic/overrides, versioned and aliased previews, `workers.dev`, routes, custom domains, service bindings, dispatch namespaces, Durable Objects, queue/scheduled/email/tail/workflow triggers, and unknown provider kinds. A provider-enforced `WorkerOverrideBarrierV1` blocks the version-override header on every production route/custom domain, permits it only through one temporary Access-protected staging hostname with a claim-bound broker-held provider token plus one-use operation grants, and proves every service-binding caller strips or denies the header. The exact attested version promotes or Unit 51 immediately restores the previous 100% deployment and proves the candidate unreachable through every external/internal invocation surface; only then can the queued previous-installed mutation replay idempotently. Pre-provenance binary attestation is explicit.
- [ ] Shared Photo Studio scenarios independently prove browser, iPhone TestFlight, iPad candidate, signed macOS candidate, deterministic MCP, and bounded agent experience where required, using a common backend oracle and run-owned data.
- [ ] Exact-manifest cleanup removes deletable run-owned D1/R2/OAuth/media/fixture/artifact/branch/worktree residue; crash-consistent supervisor-signed local tombstones and nonce-bound source-runner cleanup attestations prove final private residue zero; non-deletable provider records and preserved ownership are explicit.
- [ ] Named Worker/capability/migration/native-candidate/Release Set rollback and containment commands produce verified receipts.
- [ ] Negative proof injects stale/mismatched evidence and fails before a successful finalization.
- [ ] The closed proof-node disposition union is `required | no_op | deferred | waived | superseded | rolled_back | BLOCKED_HUMAN`; `released_with_waiver` is never a node disposition. Only `required` with fresh green proof or `no_op` with executable impact proof is shipment-eligible. A policy-authorized `ProductChangeReleasedWithWaiver` append is a separate, policy-time-bounded, non-shipping composite terminal: all current mutations are authoritatively rolled back or contained, the same CAS proves the successor ID absent and creates its canonical empty genesis, the current Product Change can never finalize or project, and the distinct successor begins with zero authorization, generation, claim, receipt, or proof state.
- [ ] `ReleaseSetPublished` is the direct child of `FinalizationClaim`, consumes independently verified cleanup plus a bounded pre-CAS point-in-time provider snapshot, contains the complete Release Set, and is the sole shipment event. It becomes effective only with exact post-result `PublicationObserved` proof; no claim is made about provider age at the unknowable atomic Git ref-update instant.
- [ ] Finalization registration preserves one attempt-specific recovery identity/public key and atomically rotates only its pre-registration grants into a closed `FinalizationRegisteredRecoveryAuthorityV1` set: strict protected-ledger-ref GET, exact parent-bound `FinalizationPreClaimCloseStartedV1`, claim-independent supervisor cleanup, exact receipt/nonreachability-bound `FinalizationPreClaimClosedV1`, and nonreachability queries. Unit 61 is wake-only; registered recovery alone invokes those close/cleanup/closed routes; coordinator authentication is typed and non-consuming; signed receipt, complete nonreachability, and registered-recovery teardown remain executable across every crash and response-loss boundary.
- [ ] A separate dormant `FinalizationClaimRecoveryAuthorityV1` is frozen before plan with zero operational/mutating grants but one immutable `ClaimRecoveryObserverGrantV1` limited to the strict no-cache protected-ledger ref plus public commit/tree/blob ancestry GET closure, and one fixed mTLS endpoint. Every operational call validates the current head's complete exact claim/operation descendant chain: before claim it rejects typed/non-consuming; exact claim atomically makes registered methods reject and the preinstalled claim-recovery predicates accept without provider-side route/grant activation. Its independent observer loop initializes/reconciles the frozen operation after coordinator death and continues across later authorized ledger descendants. Claimed methods are limited to operation status, validator/mailbox recovery, next-relay-generation authorization, exact broker-mediated foundation recovery, non-candidate-publishing recovery terminals, non-ledger observation request, and teardown; pre-claim close keeps them denied and supervisor-purges the service. Direct provider access, package/candidate bytes, exact-candidate `ReleaseSetPublished` append, arbitrary foundation, and general ledger access reject.
- [ ] Closed `ClaimRecoveryLedgerTerminalSetV1` permits claim recovery to append only predicate-bound `ValidatorRecoveryCompletedV1`, `RecoveryRepairActivatedV1`, `RecoveryUpgradeObservedV1`, and safe `FinalizationAborted`; exact-candidate `ReleaseSetPublished` remains relay-only. Disjoint `ClaimRecoveryObservationRouteV1` permits only a request to Guardian for the non-ledger `PublicationObserved` check/artifact after independently queried exact relay publication, every applicable recovery-ledger terminal, and complete operation/foundation nonreachability; it has no Ledger Writer parent/event/token semantics. Every unlisted event/request, arbitrary byte/parent, missing receipt/nonreachability predicate, or wrong state rejects typed/non-consuming.
- [ ] Fresh architecture, security, compatibility, test, release, and visual reviewers converge with no BLOCKER or MAJOR findings.
- [ ] Desk/repository records contain exact source SHAs, three digests, provider attestations, Worker/native/ASC identities, installed proof, cleanup proof, and authoritative ledger commit.

## Code Coverage Requirements

**MANDATORY: 100% coverage on all new code.**
- No coverage exclusions or warning suppression on new delivery, web, workflow-support, or native core logic.
- Every statement, branch, function, line, error path, retry, parser failure, transition, race, predicate alternative, redaction, provider failure, rollback, supersession, and cleanup refusal is tested.
- Edge cases include null, empty, duplicate, Unicode, numeric boundary, expiry boundary, malformed SHA/digest/ID, unknown field/type, ambiguous predicate, stale read, concurrent append, partial provider failure, and leaked evidence.
- Full repository suites and changed-code coverage remain green with zero warnings before each merge.

## TDD Requirements

**Strict TDD - no exceptions:**
1. **Tests first**: Write failing tests before implementation.
2. **Verify failure**: Run the focused command and capture the expected red result in private `0600` ephemeral storage. Before Unit 6c, commit no command transcript or fixture output; after Unit 6c, publish only scanner-approved summaries/digests.
3. **Minimal implementation**: Write only enough code to satisfy the contract.
4. **Verify pass**: Re-run focused tests and capture green evidence under the same private-before-Unit-6c/public-after-scan rule.
5. **Refactor**: Keep behavior and coverage green.
6. **No skipping**: Workflow, settings, shell, Swift, and live-provider behavior require executable contract tests, not prose assertions.

The Worker graph requires explicit false source config for preview URLs and `workers.dev`, byte-equal authoritative before/after inventories, and a barrier established before candidate upload: every production route/custom domain has a provider-enforced default-deny for the version-override header; one run-owned staging hostname is protected by one exact Access service-auth application/policy and a claim-bound broker-held provider token; every staged invocation consumes a distinct one-use broker authorization without exposing that token; every service-binding caller strips or rejects the header; and the exact header value/version ID are forbidden from public evidence. Correct-version hostile probes must fail without the broker identity on every external and internal path, while one authorized staging probe succeeds. Capability/order ambiguity, unknown surface, or inability to prove denial before upload requires redesign and a new reviewed graph; it never degrades to version-ID secrecy. Promotion removes the staging exception and revokes the provider token only after the candidate is normal production, while failure removes the candidate from deployment membership before barrier teardown; the permanent production-header denial remains. Key graphs bind Cloudflare secret prestate, provisional/active event generation, canonical X25519 key/fingerprint, exact Started commit, byte-identical repository/generation/key/fingerprint plus challenge/local-zero receipts in Provisioned, exact challenge/ordinary OIDC allowlists and non-consuming wrong-kind branches, active-run retention, bootstrap credential, delete/contain alternatives, and terminal. Identity registration permits only metadata-only/no-webhook/no-key/no-OAuth/no-bypass creation, records distinct provider IDs, and on ambiguity proves exact inert identities or removes them. Root installation is an ordered `rootAttemptGeneration` DAG with separate nodes for one-time journal genesis, later fast-forward attempt starts, each App elevation, each App key import, signer-policy activation, workflow permissions, merged-branch deletion, main rules, ledger rules, environment, tag rules, and ledger genesis. Generation 1 alone creates the absent journal and appends `RootAttemptStarted(1)`; generation N>1 consumes exact `RootAttemptAborted(N-1)` at the journal head and appends `RootAttemptStarted(N)` with byte-equal intent/prestate without replaying genesis. Every node has exact prestate/request/response-loss/post-query/rollback; failure appends `RootAttemptAborted`, increments generation, and cannot continue. Elevation permits only intent-bound Ledger Writer delivery `contents:write` plus `actions:read`/`checks:read`, and Guardian `actions:read`/`contents:read`/`checks:write`. Repository targets are exactly workflow `{read,false}` and `delete_branch_on_merge:true`. Guardian check emission accepts only exact workflow provenance and a canonical valid unconsumed reviewer envelope; it cannot append ledger. Source cutover binds exact root/retirement events, source identities/prestates/targets, journaled resume/rollback/containment, and terminal `SourceGuardianCutoverCompleted`. Post-root upgrade binds exact root event and all live settings/broker prestates, permits reviewed deltas/rollback/disable containment, and forbids root/genesis changes. Only `GENESIS_CREATED` permits `RootAuthorityInstalled`; its direct-child `RootSignerRetired` proves root private-key deletion before source cutover. Candidate selection retains documented total-order/fail-closed rules.

### ⬜ Unit 11b: Delivery CI and Settings Contracts - Implementation
**Required-check authority**: `ProtectedMainV1` does not trust a GitHub Actions check from `{context, Actions App ID}` alone. Keep Administration permission away from Ledger Writer. A distinct root-authorized local governance observer uses one operator credential/session for only no-cache `GET /user`, exact collaborator-permission GET, and policy-pinned ruleset GET. It requires exact provider-returned actor/collaborator ID/login, built-in `admin` permission/role, three fresh `200`s, bound bodies/Date/request IDs, complete bypass actors, and stable ruleset ETag before signing canonical one-use `ProtectedReleaseTagSnapshotV1`; cache hits, `304`, identity/role/time drift, and any other route fail. Its broker validates `release-tag-project-v1` against fresh matching pre/post snapshots, requires the canonical annotated tag ref already exist before Release POST, creates an exact body-only empty-assets GitHub Release with fixed note/latest/discussion settings, and exposes no update/delete/force/general-tag/release-asset endpoint.
**What**: Add SHA-pinned CI/resolver. Define `ProtectedMainV1` with repository-specific `{context, expected Guardian App ID}` required checks and zero classic/ruleset bypass principals or App allowances, and `ProtectedLedgerV1` with exactly the policy-pinned Ledger Writer App bypass and no ordinary writer path; both normalize/stability-check all layers. Add strict repository-settings schemas/adapters for exact no-cache `GET/PUT /repos/spoonjoy/spoonjoy-delivery/actions/permissions/workflow` and exact no-cache `GET` plus bounded `PATCH /repos/spoonjoy/spoonjoy-delivery`; only `read`/`false` and `delete_branch_on_merge: true` are valid target states. Implement a strict semantic workflow scanner that reads every exact protected-tree workflow blob, rejects duplicate/aliased/expressive permission syntax and `pull_request_target`, resolves explicit top-level and job-level effective permissions plus every same-repository relative reusable-workflow edge from that same exact tree, rejects external/dynamic reusable-workflow calls, and emits canonical `WorkflowTokenPolicyV1` with sorted workflow/job/scope inventories, blob/reusable-closure digests, and a policy digest. Allow only explicit `read` scopes and policy-bound job-specific `id-token: write` or `attestations: write`; reject all repository-mutating writes including `actions`, `checks`, `contents`, `deployments`, `discussions`, `issues`, `packages`, `pages`, `pull-requests`, `repository-projects`, `security-events`, and `statuses`. Journal expected settings prestate, request digest, dispatch uncertainty, repeated GET reconciliation, exact post-state, rollback/containment, complete workflow scan, and root/replay binding. Implement serialized broker identity/actor/transition validation, `ReviewerEnvelopeV1` verification and atomic nonce consumption, source-cutover transition validation, plus distinct web/native envelope-key generations and one-use unwrap. Canonical recipient key is raw RFC 7748 32-byte X25519 u-coordinate in base64url without padding; fingerprint is lowercase-hex SHA-256 of those bytes. Capsule encryption is RFC 9180 base-mode HPKE suite `{KEM=X25519-HKDF-SHA256(0x0020),KDF=HKDF-SHA256(0x0001),AEAD=ChaCha20Poly1305(0x0003)}` with fresh ephemeral KEM key and sequence zero. Strict RFC 8785 envelope is exactly `{version:1,suite,enc,ciphertext,payloadDigest,context}` with 32-byte `enc` and base64url-no-padding fields. `info` is `UTF8("spoonjoy-supervisor-capsule-v1") || 0x00 || RFC8785({repository,workflow,keyGeneration})`; AAD binds owner/repository/workflow/ref/SHA/run/attempt/checkRun/job/environment/claim/generation/nonce/expiry/payloadDigest/signerPublicKey. `payloadDigest` hashes canonical plaintext; claim separately binds final-envelope SHA-256. Reject reused `enc`/nonce/context, caller AEAD nonce, and noncanonical encoding. Normal unwrap requires exact active Provisioned event whose `startedCommit`, generation, canonical key, fingerprint, challenge receipt, and prior local-zero receipt validate against Started/broker state; validate OIDC/JTI, consume the ordinary nonce only after kind/endpoint checks, return signer bytes once, and reject replay/retired/contained. Provisional endpoint requires exact Started event/claim/generation/key/fingerprint/challenge job/nonce, checks capsule kind before nonce consumption, decrypts only fixed challenge once, and cannot unwrap signer capsules. Typed wrong-kind rejection uses disjoint nonce namespaces and consumes neither the challenge nor ordinary capsule. Challenge and local-zero receipts bind start commit/key/fingerprint; only an exactly preserving Provisioned transition containing both receipts activates ordinary unwrap. Timeout/crash deletes binding and appends Aborted or contains all unwraps. App key never leaves broker; envelope private key exists first only in the isolated provisioning context, then broker storage, and local purge/fsync proof must precede Provisioned. Model append state independently from credential lease; pre-mint containment, returned expiry/fingerprint persistence, `finally` revocation `204` or expiry containment, tokenless `H`, fresh-token abort CAS, and `cas_uncertain` persistence remain mandatory.

After dispatch, replacement relays preserve bindings. Same candidate remains observable after deadline; only safe close/withdrawal aborts.

The provider query starts in the same process within `0..5000` monotonic milliseconds after `S` completes. Green requires `S<=C`, `C-S<=45000`, `-5000<=R-C<=5000`, and `max(C)-min(S)<=60000`. **CAS timing protocol**: Each write invocation has one epoch with a wall-clock anchor and same-process monotonic offsets. `E`, `F`, and `H` are closed response/timeout/transport-error unions. Initial `E` has a 10000ms bound and must be fresh exact-head `200`; normalized ages against `S`, `C`, and `R` are <=360000 with only `[-5000,0]` clamped. A replacement epoch first obtains no-cache anchor `A` within 10000, verifies head, then starts `H` within `0..5000` and requires `-5000<=H.date-A.date<=20000`; no monotonic value crosses epochs. Exactly Ledger Writer creates the absent canonical nested annotated tag once under active `ProtectedReleaseTagV1`; object/ref split failure either reuses the exact OID or terminates with a preserved-provider record and no Release. The exact closed Release request sets notes/latest false and omits target/discussions. An observed response proves its real `201` metadata while treating response target as informational; a lost response can succeed only through the disjoint two-round full provider-state reconciliation and must not synthesize unavailable POST evidence. Equal body with wrong author/tag-chain/discussion/immutable/latest/timestamp/assets/identity contains; response target alone can neither authorize nor invalidate an otherwise exact chain; nonempty/duplicate assets and every update/delete/force/upload route fail. The body-only empty-assets GitHub Release identifies Unit 64c's publication and Unit 65c's pointer without becoming latest or creating a discussion; matching fresh privileged pre/post bundles and terminal token handling complete before terminal; no private artifact and no alternate shipment authority.
Timestamp acceptance additionally preserves `created_at` beside the exact peeled commit/tag dates without granting it target or operation authority, and proves `published_at` against the actual publication window in both response unions; swapped, conflated, equality-invented, or `created_at`-authorized outcomes contain.

### ⬜ Unit 67: Post-Shipment Read-Only Closeout
**What**: Only after Unit 64c, Unit 65a, Unit 65b, Unit 65c, Unit 65d, and Unit 66 prove effective observed publication and projections, read-only verify ledger/observation/main/tag/Release/providers/feedback/in-flight state. When repair was used, independently re-query `RecoveryUpgradeObservedV1`, its exact `BrokerFoundationV1`, and the full Worker invocation surface to confirm operation and foundation route/dispatch bindings/secrets are absent, foundation storage/runtime/bootstrap identity is restored or deleted, and the repair version is deactivated or expiry-contained and unreachable. Privately enumerate cleanup paths/handles/caches; update Desk/lessons/docs; retire clean task-owned branches/worktrees; notify Slugger; report public-safe inventory.
**Output**: Final evidence index; repair-foundation and broker nonreachability recheck when applicable; Desk completion commit; post-query; worktree cleanup receipt; public-safe inventory; private path-map deletion proof; and Slugger receipt.
**Acceptance**: Guard rejects aborted/non-published generations, missing repair foundation/teardown, or reachable repair runtime/storage/route/bootstrap/secret/capability; zero in-flight operation or residual task branch/worktree; repos clean/synced; unrelated work untouched; no post-shipment corrective mutation.

## Execution

- **TDD strictly enforced**: tests -> red -> implementation -> green -> refactor.
- Commit after each a/b/c phase or atomic live-operation receipt; push every commit.
- Run each repository's focused gate during development and full gate before merge/finalization.
- Before Unit 6c, store every raw transcript and hostile fixture only in private `0600` ephemeral storage and commit no log or transcript. Unit 6c may backfill only scanner-approved public summaries/digests under `./2026-07-20-1958-doing-cross-client-delivery/` after proving repository-history absence, then must purge the retained raw bytes. Later raw evidence remains private ephemeral and is deleted after sanitized proof.
- Before Unit 13b, only the isolated delivery-control-plane mutations explicitly enumerated by Unit 11c1, Unit 12c1, Unit 12c2, Unit 12c3, Unit 12c4, Unit 12c5, Unit 12c6, Unit 12c7, Unit 12c8, Unit 12c9, Unit 12c10, Unit 12c11, Unit 12c12, Unit 12c13, and Unit 12d are allowed. Every secret import in that sequence requires an exact reviewed `BrokerFoundationV1` terminal first; an empty namespace, implicit runtime, or partially created Worker/Durable Object/storage/route/bootstrap identity grants no import authority. Source edits and source/provider-release mutations are forbidden; the active TestFlight task retains exclusive web/native/production Cloudflare/ASC/TestFlight/build/notification/source-worktree and source-owned broker ownership until protected handoff.
- Provider mutations require authority, dry-run/preflight, protected ledger claim, singleton environment approval, per-request drift checks, receipts, terminal/containment, and post-query.
- The first-install bootstrap family (`github-environment-ui-bootstrap-v1` plus same-target-only resume) and restore-only `github-environment-ui-recovery-v1` are the only operations exempt from their target environment's approval gate: all require protected ledger authorization/claim, authenticated actor-ID proof, bounded UI changes, UI/API evidence, and unambiguous terminal state; none can invoke source/provider mutation code. Bootstrap installs a new gate once; recovery requires proven drift and can only restore the last attested policy. All policy changes use the installed gate.
- Cross-provider publication uses exact, age-bounded, source-owned point-in-time attestations plus a durable protected coordinator; it must never claim an atomic distributed snapshot or lock across Cloudflare and ASC because those control planes expose no shared transaction. Later external changes create new state and require a new Product Change.
- Root-policy-governed `ReplayPassGenesisV1` atomically creates/opens the sole first p1 from absent pointer and zero history with `ReplayManifestCursorV1` at the first original ID; subsequent passes use resolved/repair-completed supersession and a fresh first-node cursor. `ReplayNodeIdentityV1` maps each original Unit ID to one executor, but original IDs alone key replay state and cursor. Each `ReplayNodeSetV1` member claims only the exact cursor node through root-duration `ReplayNodeClaimV1` after proving a consumed prefix, unclaimed suffix, and no other claimed/recovering/terminal node. At original Unit 32, Unit 33, Unit 34, and Unit 35 with current-pass retained staged state, green consumption and staged-Worker cleanup activation are exclusive same-parent successors; a cleanup winner must finish node-specific cleanup, authoritative read proof, and cleanup terminal before containment, while a green winner stales only its staged-Worker sibling. Original Unit 34 first proves all prior queue authorities terminal, creates one pass/generation-bound `InstalledQueueRecoveryAuthorityV1`, and leaves it `seeded_pending` on green. A non-green original Unit 35, Unit 36, Unit 51, Unit 37, Unit 37a, Unit 38, or Unit 39 must terminally reconcile that queue before replay failure/checkpoint; device unavailability keeps the cursor recovering and forbids a fresh seed. Original Unit 36 later fences those four Worker activation routes only when current-pass Unit 60c31 retained a fresh available generation, while preserving one dormant original Unit 51 route and the separate queue authority. Its green terminal retires the Worker generation; its non-green terminal carries both Worker and queue contexts through the sole pending bridge, advances only to Unit 51, and leaves later suffix unclaimed. Unit 51 cleanup resolves both contexts before blocking its suffix and failing the pass. Unit 60c39 success alone replays and terminally retires the queue generation. If Unit 60c31 is no-replay against prior terminal Worker retirement, zero retained candidate, and zero current-pass queue, original Unit 36 must also be no-replay and consumes without a fence. Every activated pass, including an all-no-replay pass, traverses its full manifest and Unit 60c59 before resolution. A replay inherits original acceptance; failed or missing proof blocks Unit 60d and Unit 61.
- `LiveStageNodeSetV1` is the exact ordered live sequence Unit 31, Unit 32, Unit 33, Unit 34, Unit 35, Unit 36, Unit 51, Unit 37, Unit 37a, Unit 38, Unit 39, Unit 40, Unit 41, Unit 42, Unit 43, Unit 44, Unit 45, Unit 46, Unit 47, Unit 48, Unit 49, Unit 50, Unit 52, Unit 53, Unit 54, Unit 54a, Unit 55, Unit 56, Unit 57, Unit 58a, Unit 58b, and Unit 59. `LiveStageCheckpointIndexV1` contains only generic-failure causal IDs and therefore excludes Unit 36. Its exact web, native, and safety key sets and values are generated once by Unit 5s5b and reused everywhere; no stage routes backward. Unit 31 retained success freezes candidate-specific `StagedWorkerCleanupAuthorityV1`, a permanent exact-purpose mutation broker, and independent `StagedWorkerReadAuthorityV1`, each with one run-scoped ledger-gated generation containing four causal routes and one dormant Unit 51 route. At Unit 32, Unit 33, Unit 34, and Unit 35, staged-Worker cleanup activation and green terminal/consumption are exclusive successors of that causal node's parent. Cleanup runs mutation and node reconciliation independently; green permanently stales only its staged-Worker sibling. Unit 34 additionally freezes separate `InstalledQueueRecoveryAuthorityV1` before seed; green leaves it `seeded_pending` and executable. The GET-only read authority alone proves exact Worker provider state, and its reconciler's cleanup-terminal CAS retires the Worker generation. Unit 36 later fences the four Worker causal routes only at its legal cursor. Green promotion retires the dormant Worker route but preserves queue authority; a non-green Unit 36 can only append `LiveStageContainmentPendingV1` carrying both contexts, activate Unit 51, and wait. Unit 51 resolves both Worker and queue cleanup before emitting causal Unit 51 `LiveStageFailedV1`; a failure at Unit 35, Unit 37, Unit 37a, Unit 38, or Unit 39 likewise requires queue terminal first. Unit 39 success alone replays and retires the queue generation. No mutation broker may certify its own cleanup, no retained resource or installed queue may lose an executable owner, and no blocked successor may call a provider.
- Finalization registration reservation, registration dispatch, and claim are separate protected transitions under one root-bound `FinalizationResolverLockV1`. Before any ledger call, an attempt-specific independently restartable `FinalizationPreRegistrationRecoveryAuthorityV1` fsyncs `FinalizationRegistrationPlanV1`; the coordinator then CASes on-ledger `FinalizationRegistrationAttemptV1`. An exact divergent successor proves the reservation lost before registration dispatch and permits plan-only cleanup, while an unchanged or ambiguous parent remains recovery-owned. Exact attempt admits only `FinalizationRecoveryRegistered` or sibling `FinalizationPreRegistrationCloseStartedV1`; registered-open admits only `FinalizationClaimDispatchIntentV1` or sibling `FinalizationPreClaimCloseStartedV1`; claim-intent-open admits only exact claim or sibling close-start. A strict attempt-head anchor derives the root-bound lease; fsynced `FinalizationRegistrationDispatchIntentV1` precedes registration. Destructive cleanup after dispatch intent waits for one observed sibling winner. If pre-registration close-start wins with zero registration/claim, the pre-registration authority survives coordinator death through supervisor cleanup, signed receipt reconciliation, exact `FinalizationPreRegistrationClosedV1`, complete nonreachability, and ordered teardown. If registration wins, it preserves the exact attempt-specific identity/public key and atomically replaces only pre-registration grants with `FinalizationRegisteredRecoveryAuthorityV1` grants. Unit 61 may only wake that identity; after winning pre-claim close-start plus zero claim, registered recovery alone invokes supervisor cleanup, reconciles the signed receipt, appends exact `FinalizationPreClaimClosedV1`, proves complete nonreachability, and tears itself down. Coordinator credentials are typed/non-consuming rejects, recovery cannot clean directly, and the supervisor cannot append. Coordinator, recovery, and supervisor crashes converge at every boundary. Only a confirmed claim-intent response may obtain a strict exact-intent-head anchor satisfying `registrationIssuedAtDateMs <= intentAnchorDateMs <= registrationDeadlineMs-5000`, and that anchor alone launches claim within `0..5000` monotonic milliseconds. Exact claim or exact authorized close releases the lock. Exact claim alone permits Durable Object operation creation; either exact close-start permits only its matching registration-independent or claim-independent cleanup and null-generation closure. Unrelated drift is a root-integrity failure, never a close cause.
- Canonical `FinalizationRegisteredRecoveryPolicyV1` closes the post-registration liveness graph: the same attempt-specific identity receives only strict protected-ledger-ref GET, exact parent-bound `FinalizationPreClaimCloseStartedV1`, claim-independent supervisor cleanup, receipt/nonreachability-bound exact `FinalizationPreClaimClosedV1`, and nonreachability queries. Registered recovery alone owns both Ledger Writer methods and cleanup reconciliation; Unit 61 is wake-only. Coordinator credentials and registered claim-intent, claim, direct-cleanup, package/source/provider, or general-ledger calls are typed/non-consuming rejects. Missing routes, grant overlap/gaps, and teardown before exact closed plus complete nonreachability fail.
- Canonical `FinalizationClaimRecoveryPolicyV1` closes the post-claim liveness graph through a distinct identity frozen dormant before plan. Exact claim atomically retires registered recovery and activates only frozen operation initialization/status, validator/mailbox recovery, next-relay-generation authorization, exact broker-mediated foundation recovery, non-candidate-publishing recovery terminals, and teardown; pre-claim close purges the dormant identity. Claim recovery has no provider credential/direct route, package/candidate bytes, exact-candidate `ReleaseSetPublished` append, source rerun, arbitrary foundation, or general ledger method. Claim-response loss and coordinator death preserve one owner through terminal operation/foundation nonreachability and teardown.
- The pre-registration recovery supervisor-invocation predicate is a closed two-branch union. Before an attempt lands, only complete protected ancestry excluding the deterministic attempt OID plus zero dispatch intent/registration/claim authorizes plan-only cleanup. After an attempt lands, only exact close-start plus zero registration/claim authorizes registration-independent cleanup. Both remain owned after coordinator death through signed cleanup receipt, exact `FinalizationPreRegistrationClosedV1`, complete nonreachability, and recovery teardown; neither can substitute evidence from the other.
- Every ordinary replay consumption CAS stores immutable `ReplayNodeConsumedV1` with pass/original-node/attempt/decision/result/manifest/pre-post-cursor/transition/ledger bindings. Immediate-poststate equality is sufficient but not necessary: an exact receipt plus a gap-free same-pass same-manifest receipt chain may prove a monotonic descendant current state through later node activity, containment, completion, resolution, or supersession. Recovery never advances a receipted node twice, and pass consumption requires the complete ordered receipt chain.
- Public-safe records are committed on the named iteration branch and merge through protected PR/CI checkpoints after Unit 29e, Unit 42c, Unit 50c, Unit 59c, and Unit 60f3. A generic live failure invokes only its stage's forward checkpoint: web through Unit 42c, native through Unit 50c, or safety through Unit 59c. Finalization consumes only Unit 60f3's exact protected-main commit and GitHub attestation; branch-only records are never authoritative.
- Visual changes or consuming-surface proof require `visual-qa-dogfood` and a closed absurdity ledger.
- Fix ordinary blockers with fresh sub-agents and TDD; surface only true human-only credentials, hardware, or account capability once safe authenticated paths are exhausted.
- Keep checklists and ledger/Desk ownership current after every transition.
